- **Depends:** U50, G10. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-ci changed:: affected:: warm:: rules::`
- **Brief:** `.asc/ci.toml` (read via `ReadFile` at the merge base, or `--rules-file <local path>`; any change to it sets every switch true and `all=true`, SPEC §12.2) [audit: plan-job-rules-file-without-checkout] schema: `[switches]` (globs, or `{ projects = ["<selector>", ...] }`) and `[outputs]` names; no `turbo` keys. `changed`: one `Diff(NAMES, merge_base)` → switches, plus `semantic=<overall>` from `Classify` (U67) when the server offers it. `affected`: calls the G10 client with `--format gha` and adds the project-selector switches; on RPC failure it writes `all=true`, sets every switch true, and warns (SPEC §12.2). `warm`: fetch trunk changesets, trees and files into the host cache, with 0–30 s jitter and `--seed-from` (a systemd timer unit file template is included in `deploy/`); `asc_cache_requests_total{tier="host"}` and `asc_warm_*` metrics. Tests: rules evaluation; selector switches; the failure fallback; rules read without a checkout; a PR that edits `.asc/ci.toml` gets every switch true.

### U75 asc ci cache/artifact (Actions fork backend) [audit: user decision Actions fork]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-ci/src/{cache.rs,artifact.rs}`, `crates/asc/src/cli/ci/{cache,artifact}.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/src/actions_store.rs`
- **Depends:** U62, U49, O01. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-ci cache:: artifact:: && cargo test -p asc-serve actions_store::`
- **Brief [audit: actions-cache-artifact-underspecified, actions-fork-contract-and-naming]:** exactly SPEC §12.7's store contract. `asc ci cache save|restore <key> [--restore-keys …] <paths>` and `asc ci artifact upload|download|list <name> <paths>` upload file content with `HasObjects`/`PutObjects` into the repo namespace (FileIds; chunked over 4 MiB) and write an `ActionsManifest { version: u8 = 1, entries: Vec<(RepoPath, Kind, FileId, u64)> }` (postcard) through `PUT /actions/v1/<owner>/<repo>/<cache|artifact>/<key>`; `GET` on the same path, and `GET …/cache?prefix=<restore-key>` (newest first). `actions_store.rs` derives the scope from the token (SPEC §12.4), stores manifests at `actions/<owner>.<repo>/<cache|artifact>/<scope>/<pct(key)>`, applies §12.4 retention, and implements O01's `GcRootSource` (every unexpired manifest's FileIds). Tests: an identical file in two caches is stored once; PR scopes cannot write `trusted`; `actions_store::restore_keys_prefix` (prefix matching equals `actions/cache` semantics); `actions_store::gc_root` (O01's sweep keeps the files of an unexpired manifest).

### U76 `WatchRefs` ref-update stream (live updates server side)
- **Owns:** `proto/asc/v1/repo.proto` (additive hunk, recorded), `crates/asc-serve/src/watch_refs.rs`, `crates/asc-rpc/src/watch_refs.rs`
- **Depends:** U42, U43b, U47, B08a. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-serve watch_refs:: && cargo test -p asc-rpc watch_refs::`
- **Brief:** SPEC §21.3.8. Server-streaming `WatchRefs(repo, patterns, resume)` over the ref-update outbox of U43b; client in `asc-rpc` with `resume` cursors. Each event is filtered by the caller's ACL (§15.1) and by `patterns` (at most 8 globs, each at most 128 bytes, default `master` and `release/*`); a user-ref pattern matches the caller's own refs only. Tests: a caller without read access receives no event and no name leak; another user's draft ref never appears; a burst of N updates to one ref delivers in order and `resume` replays with no gap and no duplicate; an over-limit pattern list returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT`; the stream closes on token expiry.

### U77 Client credential resolver, git-compatible (`asc_git::credential`, `asc::credential`) [SPEC §15.6.1]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-git/src/credential.rs` (extends the built `fill`; recorded amendment), `crates/asc-git/src/credential/{netrc.rs,urlmatch.rs}`, `crates/asc/src/credential.rs`, `crates/asc/tests/credential.rs`
- **Depends:** U16a, U20. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-git credential:: && cargo test -p asc --test credential`
- **Brief:** SPEC §15.6.1, the one resolver for every asc transport. State today (checked 2026-10-23): `asc_git::credential::fill(real, protocol, host)` runs `git credential fill` through `RealGit`, with no caller, no `approve`/`reject`, no path or username attributes, and it leaves `GIT_ASKPASS` and `SSH_ASKPASS` as they are; `crates/asc/src/cli/fetch.rs` and `cli/auth.rs` are stubs (U32, U50); `RpcClient::connect(url, repo, Option<BearerToken>)` takes whatever token its caller found and sends only `Bearer`; S3 remotes get injected `S3Credentials` (CARRY); git-remote mode needs nothing, because real git authenticates. `asc_git::credential`: `CredentialRequest { protocol, host, port, path, username, wwwauth }`; `fill`, `approve` and `reject` through `RealGit::command()` (never `git` by name); `Prompt::{Never, Allowed}` where `Never` sets `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, `GCM_INTERACTIVE=never`, `credential.interactive=false` and clears `GIT_ASKPASS`, `SSH_ASKPASS` and `core.askPass`; strict value checks (NUL, CR and LF refused in a request and in an answer); `authtype`/`credential`, `capability[]`, `wwwauth[]` and `password_expiry_utc` used only when real git supports them; `Credential` keeps its redacted `Debug`. `netrc.rs` reads `~/.netrc` (`_netrc` on Windows): `machine`, `default`, `login`, `password`, `macdef` skipped. `urlmatch.rs` is git's URL matching (scheme, host with `*` labels, port, path prefix, user; most specific wins) for `http.<url>.extraHeader` and `credential.<url>.*`. `asc::credential::resolve(&Config, &Url, Prompt) -> Resolved` follows the SPEC order exactly (extra header, URL userinfo, `ASC_TOKEN`, netrc, helpers, prompt); `Resolved` carries the scheme (Basic or Bearer), the user name, the secret, the origin it is bound to and its `source()` name; `report_result(&Resolved, accepted)` calls `approve` once on success and `reject` on a 401, only for credentials that `fill` produced; `same_origin` and `redirect_policy` give the SPEC host-binding rules (drop `Authorization` and extra headers across origins, https to http refused, at most 5 hops, none for RPC) to U50, U72's seed download and the `objects` tier. No `login` command exists. Tests (fake helpers are scripts that log every invocation and its attributes): each source alone and in SPEC order; `credential.helper` lists, the empty-value reset, `credential.<url>.helper` precedence, `useHttpPath` on and off (the `path` attribute), `credential.username`; `https://TOKEN@host` becomes Basic `TOKEN:`; `ASC_TOKEN` is used for the remote's origin and its `objects` hosts and for no other host; `Prompt::Never` never starts an askpass program (a marker script with `GIT_ASKPASS` set stays unrun) and `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0` is honoured under `Allowed`; `approve` after acceptance, `reject` after a 401, neither for sources 1 to 4; a redirect to another origin drops the credential and asks again; plain http is refused except to loopback; CR, LF or NUL in a helper answer is rejected; a canary secret never appears in any `Debug`, `Display`, error, trace or op-log output; `#[cfg(windows)] #[ignore]` smoke for `manager`.

### U78 Server credential edge: Basic, Bearer and `token`, and the `WWW-Authenticate` challenge (`asc-rpc::auth`, `asc-serve::authn`) [SPEC §15.6.2]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-serve/src/authn.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/tests/authn.rs`. **Recorded amendments (hunks in files other units own, allowed like the `mod` registration):** U42 `crates/asc-rpc/src/auth.rs` and `client.rs`, U44 `crates/asc-serve/src/forgejo.rs`, U70 `crates/asc-serve/src/cache_role.rs` (the admin layer), U72 `crates/asc-serve/src/seeds.rs` (the error mapping), U43c `crates/asc-serve/src/http.rs` (one layer registration)
- **Depends:** U42, U43c, U44. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-rpc auth:: && cargo test -p asc-serve --test authn`
- **Brief:** SPEC §15.6.2. Today (checked 2026-10-23): `AuthInterceptor` in `asc-rpc/src/auth.rs` accepts only `Bearer` and answers anything else `Malformed` (its test asserts that `Basic` is refused); the admin layer in `cache_role.rs` compares a `Bearer` token and answers `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer`; `seeds.rs` answers 401 with no challenge and 400 for `Malformed`; `RpcClient` sends only `Bearer`. This unit adds `asc_rpc::auth::{Credential, Scheme, parse_authorization}` (Basic with the secret taken from the password, or from the user name when the password is empty; Bearer; `token`; case-insensitive scheme; two headers, over 4,096 bytes or an unknown scheme is `Malformed`), keeps `BearerToken` as the secret type, and adds `Authorizer::authorize_credential(Option<&Credential>, repo, access)` whose default delegates to `authorize` with the secret (U44's forge authorizers override it to forward `user:secret` for password-equivalent logins, cached under `sha256(user NUL secret)`). `RpcClient::connect` takes `Option<Credential>` and sends the header it names. `asc_serve::authn` is the axum layer for `/v8`, `/actions/v1`, `/seeds`, `/mcp` and the admin listener (the admin token accepts Bearer or Basic, constant-time): it answers 401 with `WWW-Authenticate: Basic realm="asc <host>", charset="UTF-8"` first, adds a `Bearer` challenge where Bearer is taken (and `/mcp`'s `resource_metadata` challenge), 400 for `Malformed` and for a token in the query string, 403 for too little access, the same `NOT_FOUND` as a missing repo for an unreadable one, and 401 (not 404) for an anonymous request to a repo it cannot read, whether or not it exists. A request for `/<owner>/<repo>[.git]/info/refs`, `git-upload-pack` or `git-receive-pack` gets the challenge when anonymous and, once authenticated, the **disabled stub** of SPEC §25.9 (v1.12, replacing the v1.11 "404, the forge serves git" answer): `403` with `X-Asc-Git: disabled` and the body `asc: git protocol disabled on this server`, so a real git client pointed at `asc serve` behaves normally. U81 replaces the stub with the live layer when the feature `git-compat` is on and `[git] enabled` is true. Failed attempts are counted per source address and per `sha256(secret)` through U69's admission class. The audit record gets the method (`basic`, `bearer`, `token`, `oauth`) and the principal, never the credential. Tests: Basic with `x-access-token`, `oauth2`, `gitlab-ci-token`, empty and real user names all resolve to the same principal; `https://TOKEN@host` style (token as user name, empty password) works; the three schemes give one cache entry; every route and the gRPC interceptor share the parser (a table test over the route list fails when a route is added without the layer); the challenge headers per route; a revoked or wrong token gives the same answer as an unknown one; no response, log line or audit record contains the secret (canary); the existing U42 test is changed from `Basic` refused to `Basic` accepted and `Digest` refused.

### U79 Auth compatibility suite against real git (`crates/asc/tests/auth_compat/`) [SPEC §15.6, §17.1]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc/tests/auth_compat/{main.rs,fake_host.rs,helpers.rs}`
- **Depends:** U77, U78. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc --test auth_compat` (real git ≥ 2.40 through `RealGit`; the Windows GCM case is `#[ignore]`)
- **Brief:** Proves that asc authenticates as git does, by running both. (1) A test-only fake git host (axum) that issues git's exact flow (an unauthenticated first request, 401 with `WWW-Authenticate: Basic`, a retry with credentials) and records every request header. For each configuration, run real `git ls-remote <url>` and asc's resolver against the same host and compare the `Authorization` headers on the wire and the sequence and attributes of the helper calls (`get`, `store`, `erase`): the `store` and `cache` helpers, a `!script` recorder, a GCM-style recorder that must receive `wwwauth[]`, `credential.<url>.helper`, `useHttpPath`, `credential.username`, `.netrc`, global and URL-scoped `http.extraHeader` (GitHub Actions `actions/checkout` style and Azure DevOps `bearer` style), `GIT_ASKPASS`, `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, URL userinfo and `https://TOKEN@host`. (2) Redirects: a 302 to a second host receives no `Authorization` and no extra header from real git or from asc (asc is stricter for `http.extraHeader`; the test pins that as the documented difference). (3) Real git against the asc edge: `git ls-remote` pointed at an in-process `asc serve` (token store, started with `[git] enabled = false` so that the disabled stub answers in every build; the live layer is U83's) gets the Basic challenge, runs the helper's `get`, retries with Basic, is authorised (the audit record names the principal) and then gets the `403 git protocol disabled` stub; a revoked token gives 401 and the helper's `erase`; a read deploy token and a write deploy token are told apart on the RPC. (4) Git-remote mode: `asc clone`, `fetch` and `push` against the fake git host call the helper exactly as git does, with no extra credential request from asc. (5) CI: a repo config with `http.<url>.extraheader` set as `actions/checkout` writes it authenticates asc to the asc edge with no other setup. Credentials in every assertion message are masked.

### U80 Git compatibility module: feature `git-compat`, `ServerGit` and the git view (`asc-git::compat`) [SPEC §25.1–§25.3, §25.8–§25.9]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-git/src/compat/{mod.rs,server_git.rs,view.rs,pktline.rs,limits.rs}`, `crates/asc-git/tests/{compat_server_git.rs,compat_view.rs}`, `crates/asc-serve/src/git_view.rs`. **Recorded amendments (hunks in files other units own, allowed like the `mod` registration):** workspace `Cargo.toml` (`asc-git` becomes `default-features = false`), `crates/asc-git/Cargo.toml` (`[features] default = ["git-compat"]`, `git-compat`, the optional dependencies), `crates/asc-git/src/lib.rs` (`#[cfg(feature = "git-compat")] pub mod compat;`), `crates/asc-serve/Cargo.toml` and `crates/asc/Cargo.toml` (forwarding features, default on), U43c `crates/asc-serve/src/{config.rs,limits.rs}` (the `[git]` and `[limits.git]` tables, parsed in every build), U43c `crates/asc-serve/src/outbox.rs` (a `git_view_sync` effect, behind the feature)
- **Depends:** U33, U34, U16a, U43c. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-git --test compat_server_git --test compat_view && cargo check -p asc-git --no-default-features`
- **Brief:** SPEC §25. Everything is behind `#[cfg(feature = "git-compat")]`; nothing outside `asc-git` names `asc_git::compat` except the feature-gated wiring of `asc-serve` and `asc` (CARRY). `ServerGit::new(&RealGit, ServerGitConfig) -> ServerGit` is the only way to start a server-side git child, so the "RealGit is the only git path" lint holds: empty environment plus the fixed allow-list of SPEC §25.8 item 2 (`GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM`, empty `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL` and `GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM`, private empty `HOME`, `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, a validated `GIT_PROTOCOL`, `GIT_USER_AGENT`), every behaviour passed as `-c` (hooks path, `protocol.allow=never`, fsck, `receive.maxInputSize`, `uploadpack.allowFilter`, `transfer.hideRefs`, the full list in §25.8), child limits (memory, CPU, processes, wall and idle deadlines, process-tree kill), and a `git --version` gate (at least 2.40). `GitView`: `open(view_dir, repo_id)` creates the bare view with an empty template and rewrites its `config` from a fixed template when the hash differs; `sync(&dyn RefSource, &Exporter) -> SyncReport` exports the missing closure of the shared refs and tags (U34), sets the refs with `git update-ref --stdin`, and is a no-op when every ref is equal; `Coalesce` singleflight per repo; the build state machine (`absent`, `building`, `ready`) with the 50,000-object inline threshold and a `503` + `Retry-After` answer while building; `prune`/`rebuild` for `asc serve admin git-view {warm,rebuild,prune}`; a mirror-mode repo uses its bare mirror as the view. `pktline.rs`: a bounded pkt-line reader and writer (length at most 65,520, `0000` flush and `0001` delim handling, `ERR` packets) for the advertisement prefix (`# service=…`), the `0000` probe and the v2 capability check; it never parses a pack. `limits.rs`: the `[limits.git]` types and defaults of SPEC §25.5. Tests (real git ≥ 2.40 through `RealGit`): the child environment contains exactly the allow-list (a canary `GIT_DIR`, `GIT_CONFIG_COUNT`, `GIT_EXEC_PATH`, `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` and `HOME/.gitconfig` in the parent are never seen); a malicious `config` in the view (`core.hooksPath`, `core.fsmonitor`, `uploadpack.packObjectsHook`, `receive.denyCurrentBranch`) is rewritten and its hooks never run; `GIT_PROTOCOL` values with a space, NUL, newline or `;` are dropped; a child that exceeds the memory limit, the deadline or the idle limit is killed with its children; the view of a native history has the same commit SHAs as `git commit-tree` and as U34; `sync` after a ref move exports only the new closure; two concurrent syncs build once; a mirror-mode repo is served from its mirror dir with no export; with `--no-default-features` the crate builds and `asc_git::compat` does not exist.

### U81 Upload-pack: `info/refs` and `git-upload-pack` over smart-HTTP (`asc-git::compat::upload`, `asc-serve::git_http`) [SPEC §25.1–§25.2, §25.4–§25.5, §25.8, §25.9]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-git/src/compat/upload.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/src/git_http.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/tests/{git_upload.rs,git_disabled.rs}`. **Recorded amendments:** U78 `crates/asc-serve/src/authn.rs` (the git route stub, below), U69 admission (class `git`, `crates/asc-serve/src/limits.rs`), U43c `crates/asc-serve/src/http.rs` (route registration, behind the feature)
- **Depends:** U80, U42, U45, U69, U78. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-serve --test git_upload && cargo test -p asc-serve --no-default-features --test git_disabled`
- **Brief:** SPEC §25. Routes: `GET /<repo>[.git]/info/refs?service=git-upload-pack` and `POST /<repo>[.git]/git-upload-pack`, registered behind the U78 credential layer, so the table test "every route has the layer" lists them. Path handling is SPEC §25.8 item 1 (decode once, strict name pattern, registry lookup to the repo id, the directory built from the id, only the two services, dumb paths and `git-upload-archive` refused). Order per request: admission (class `git`; discovery is `cheap`, fetch POSTs hold a concurrency slot), U78 authentication and the `Authorizer` read check, the disabled and object-format gate, `GitView::sync`, then `ServerGit` `upload-pack --stateless-rpc [--advertise-refs]` streamed both ways with byte counting, a gzip request body inflated under the 16 MiB cap, content-type checks (`415`), the `0000` probe answered before any child starts, `Cache-Control: no-cache`, and the v2 `Git-Protocol` header forwarded only when valid. Refs hidden: `refs/pull`, `refs/users`, `refs/snapshots`. The **disabled stub** (U78's former "404, the forge serves git" answer is replaced by this contract): with the feature off, `[git] enabled = false`, git older than 2.40 or an unsupported object format, an anonymous request gets the `401` Basic challenge and an authenticated one `403` with `X-Asc-Git: disabled` and the body `asc: git protocol disabled on this server`; an unreadable repo stays `404`. The stub lives in `authn.rs` and is compiled in both builds. Audit record and metrics per SPEC §25.8 item 9. Tests with real git: `git clone`, `fetch`, `ls-remote` (v0, v1, v2 by `-c protocol.version`), `--depth 1`, `--shallow-since`, `--deepen`, `--unshallow`, `--filter=blob:none`, `--filter=tree:0` and the lazy blob fetch that follows, a tag fetch with `include-tag`; an anonymous clone of a private repo gets the challenge, then the helper's credential works; a read deploy token clones and gets `403` on the receive-pack advertisement; a revoked token gets `401`; no read access gives `404`; hidden refs never appear and a `want` for an object reachable only from a hidden ref is refused; `..`, `%2e%2e`, `%00`, `\`, `//` and a 101-byte name all get `404` and spawn no child; a pack-bomb request (a huge `have` list, a 20 MiB gzip body inflating to 2 GiB) is cut at the cap; the wall deadline kills a stalled client; the 5th concurrent fetch by one principal gets `429`; a clone of a mirror-mode repo works and its receive-pack advertisement is `403` with the read-only-mirror text; the `git_disabled` test (no default features) asserts the stub answers above and that the binary has no `compat` symbols.

### U82 Receive-pack: push through the pre-receive hook into `UpdateRefs` (`asc-git::compat::{receive,hook}`, `asc-serve::git_push`, `asc`) [SPEC §25.2, §25.4–§25.6, §25.8]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-git/src/compat/{receive.rs,hook.rs}`, `crates/asc-serve/src/git_push.rs`, `crates/asc/src/cli/serve_git_hook.rs`, `crates/asc-git/tests/compat_hook.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/tests/git_push.rs`. **Recorded amendments:** U48d `crates/asc-serve/src/import_push.rs` (the in-process entry `import_git_push(quarantine, commands, principal, opts)` that the RPC handler and this unit share; the RPC behaviour is unchanged), U81 `crates/asc-serve/src/git_http.rs` (the receive-pack routes), `crates/asc/src/cli/mod.rs` (`entry!` for the hidden `serve git-hook`, behind the feature)
- **Depends:** U81, U48d, U42, U69, U78. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-serve --test git_push && cargo test -p asc-git --test compat_hook`
- **Brief:** SPEC §25.2 (writes), §25.6, §25.8. `GET info/refs?service=git-receive-pack` needs `write` (`403` for read-only, `404` for unreadable, the `401` challenge for anonymous) and `POST git-receive-pack` streams the request into `git receive-pack --stateless-rpc` with `core.hooksPath` set to the asc-owned hooks directory that holds one fixed `pre-receive` script (read-only; it execs the absolute path of the `asc` binary with `serve git-hook pre-receive`). The hook (`hook.rs`, the `asc` subcommand) reads git's command lines, connects to the per-process Unix socket (a named pipe on Windows with the daemon's DACL rules, SPEC §9.1) in `$ASC_HOME/git-compat/run/` (mode 0700), presents the single-use 256-bit request token from its environment, forwards the quarantine variables and `GIT_PUSH_OPTION_*`, prints the server's refusal reason on stderr and exits 0 or 1. The server side (`git_push.rs`) maps the token to principal, repo, `op_id` and limits, enforces `max_push_objects` and `max_object_bytes` from the quarantine, refuses `.git` aliases with `checkout_safe` on every added or changed path in every mode, applies the §2.8 rule of SPEC §25.6 (import rule for the first push to an empty repo or `repos.git_import_grade`, native rule otherwise), runs the shared `import_git_push` under the per-repo advisory lock (U33 round trip, `changes` derivation) and then `UpdateRefs` **without** `SKIP_PRE` (the full pipeline: Forgejo pre-receive when configured, outbox `post_receive` and `bridge_promote`, `via = git-push`, the GC fence of CARRY). User-ref mapping, TTL and quota come from the pipeline and from B08a when it lands. Output is git's own report-status; refusals appear as `remote:` lines plus `pre-receive hook declined`; a user-ref push adds the `remote: asc: stored as your branch …` line (SPEC §25.6). Receive-pack config is the fixed `-c` list of §25.8 (fsck on, `receive.maxInputSize`, `unpackLimit=1`, no certificates) with the `[git] fsck = "migration"` ignore list; a mirror-mode repo is refused. Tests with real git: push of a new branch, a fast-forward, a deletion, a tag, `--atomic` (the hook is all-or-nothing), `--force` to a ref the policy forbids, a lost CAS race (two pushes, one wins, the loser sees a `remote rejected` line and nothing of it is indexed), a push option `asc.ttl=1d`, `-o land` refused; pushes of `.git/config`, `.GIT/`, `git~1/`, `.git::$INDEX_ALLOCATION` and a `.gitmodules` with a dangerous `url` or as a symlink are refused with nothing imported; the first push of a history with a `CON` file and a case collision to an empty repo is accepted, and the same push to a non-empty repo is refused unless `git_import_grade`; a pack with a delta that expands past the memory cap, a zlib bomb, 2,000,001 tiny objects and a 513 MiB blob are all refused within their limits and the server's RSS stays under the child cap; a push that dies mid-pack leaves no quarantine after the janitor run and no asc object reachable; the hook socket rejects a replayed or expired token and a token for another repo; no secret appears in a child's argv or environment (canary); the pushed commits map back to the same SHAs (`git_map` round trip) and a later `git fetch` by another clone sees them; with the fake Forgejo of `fake_forgejo.rs`, a push to a shared ref makes pre-receive and post-receive calls and a user-ref push makes none.

### U83 Git client compatibility suite and the `--no-default-features` build (`crates/asc/tests/git_compat/`) [SPEC §25.10, §17.1]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc/tests/git_compat/{main.rs,clients.rs,migrate.rs,branchless.rs}`, `scripts/ci/no-git-compat.sh`
- **Depends:** U82, U79, U77, B08a. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc --test git_compat && scripts/ci/no-git-compat.sh` (real git ≥ 2.40 through `RealGit`; the Windows GCM case is `#[ignore]`)
- **Brief:** Proves the layer by running real clients against an in-process `asc serve` (token store) and compares git's own output with its output against a plain git server (`git http-backend` behind a test axum fixture) for the same commands, so "git-identical" is measured. Cases: `git clone` (empty repo, small repo, an ASC-shaped fixture with 10,000 commits), `fetch`, `pull --ff-only`, `push` (new branch, fast-forward, rejected non-fast-forward text, `--force`, delete, tag, `--atomic`, `--mirror`), shallow (`--depth`, `--deepen`, `--unshallow`, `fetch --shallow-since`), partial clone (`--filter=blob:none`, `tree:0`, `blob:limit`) with the lazy fetch of a missing blob, `ls-remote` in protocol v0, v1 and v2, a submodule cloned through asc URLs, and the credential paths of U79 repeated against the live git layer (the `store` and `cache` helpers, `credential.<url>.helper`, GCM-style `wwwauth[]`, `.netrc`, `http.<url>.extraHeader` as `actions/checkout` writes it, `https://TOKEN@host`, `GIT_ASKPASS`, `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, a read and a write deploy token, a revoked token), plus an `actions/checkout`-style fetch of one SHA. **Migration:** `git clone --mirror` a fixture repo (merges, signed commits, an annotated tag, odd modes, an unsorted tree: the U33 fixtures) from a plain git server, `git push --mirror` it to asc, then `asc clone` it and `git clone` it back and compare every ref and SHA (zero mismatches; skipped refs listed as in SPEC §14.1). **Branchless cases** (SPEC §25.6, with B08a): `git push origin HEAD` on a non-shared branch becomes a user ref with the `remote: asc: stored as your branch` line and no PR, `git push origin HEAD:refs/for/master` creates a draft change, a push to `master` follows branch protection, a deletion removes the user ref, and the pusher's user refs are not advertised to a second principal. **Whole-stack security and limits:** the disabled-stub answers, `[git] enabled = false` at run time gives the same answers, mirror-mode repos are read-only, each request has one audit record with no credential, and admission sheds the over-limit fetch. `scripts/ci/no-git-compat.sh`: `cargo build -p asc -p asc-serve --no-default-features`, `cargo test -p asc-git -p asc-serve --no-default-features`, `cargo tree -e features -p asc --no-default-features` has no `git-compat` line, and a small test starts `asc serve` from that build and asserts the stub (`401` challenge, then `403 git protocol disabled`) for `info/refs`, `git-upload-pack` and `git-receive-pack`. Both builds must pass in CI. SSH cases (SPEC §25.11) are U88's; U88 adds the `russh` absence check to `scripts/ci/no-git-compat.sh` as a recorded amendment.

### U84 SSH public keys in the `Authorizer` and the token store (`asc-rpc::auth`, `asc-serve::authz`) [SPEC §15.6.2, §25.11.3]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-serve/src/authz/ssh_keys.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/migrations/{postgres,sqlite}/<next free number>_ssh_keys.sql`, `crates/asc-serve/tests/ssh_keys.rs`. **Recorded amendments (hunks in files other units own, allowed like the `mod` registration):** U42 `crates/asc-rpc/src/auth.rs` (the types and two trait methods below), M30 `crates/asc-serve/src/authz/{mod.rs,token_store.rs,token_cli.rs}` (wiring), the U43a `asc serve` subcommand table (one `ssh-key` line)
- **Depends:** U42, M30, U78. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-rpc auth:: && cargo test -p asc-serve --test ssh_keys`
- **Brief:** SPEC §25.11.3. `asc_rpc::auth` gains `SshKey { algo, blob }` with `fingerprint() -> SshFingerprint` (SHA-256 of the wire blob, printed `SHA256:<base64>`; no key parser in `asc-rpc`), `SshIdentity { key_id, kind: User | Deploy, principal, repo: Option<RepoId> }`, and the `Authorizer` methods `identify_ssh_key(&SshKey) -> Result<Option<SshIdentity>>` and `authorize_ssh_key(&SshKey, repo, access) -> Result<Decision>`; the defaults answer `None` and `Denied`, so every existing implementation (static table, forge) refuses SSH until it opts in. The decision uses the §15.1 cache keyed `(sha256(blob), repo, mode)`, 60 s positive and 5 s negative. `TokenStoreAuthz` implements both over the table `ssh_keys(id, kind /*user|deploy*/, principal, repo_id NULL, write BOOL, algo, public_key BLOB, fingerprint BLOB UNIQUE, title, expires, revoked, created, last_used)`: a user key resolves to its principal with the principal's own grants, a deploy key to its one repo, read unless `write`; the principal is rebuilt from typed columns as M30 does after 99f68ae, never from stored text. `asc serve ssh-key add --user <u> --key-file <pub> [--title <t>] [--expires <d>]`, `add --repo <r> --deploy [--write] --key-file <pub>`, `list`, `revoke <fingerprint|id>`: the key file is an `authorized_keys` line (type, base64, comment; any option prefix such as `command=` or `restrict` is refused) and is parsed with the key types of the SSH library (`russh::keys`), so these commands and the table code are behind `git-ssh`, while the migration and the asc-rpc types are always built. Key types and RSA size are checked at `add` with the same list as the listener (SPEC §25.11.1). Tests: add, identify, authorise, expire and revoke; a user key cannot reach a repo its user cannot; a deploy key cannot reach another repo and a read deploy key cannot write; the same key twice is refused, also across kinds; an `authorized_keys` line with options is refused; a revoked key is refused within the 5 s negative bound; the stored row holds the public blob and no private material; a row with an untyped principal is refused; `cargo check -p asc-serve --no-default-features` compiles without the CLI or table code.

### U85 Forge SSH key lookup (`asc-serve::forgejo_ssh`) [SPEC §25.11.3]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-serve/src/forgejo_ssh.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/tests/forgejo_ssh.rs`. **Recorded amendments:** U44 `crates/asc-serve/src/forgejo.rs` (one registration line) and `crates/asc-serve/tests/fake_forgejo.rs` (the two endpoints of K19)
- **Depends:** U44, U84. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-serve --test forgejo_ssh --test fake_forgejo`
- **Brief:** `InternalAuthz` (U44) implements `identify_ssh_key` with `POST /api/internal/asc/ssh-key` `{type, key (base64 blob), fingerprint}` and `authorize_ssh_key` with the `ssh_key` variant of `POST /api/internal/asc/authz` `{ssh_key, repo, mode}`, both with `INTERNAL_TOKEN`; the answers are K07's (`allowed`, `user_id`, `username`, `perm`, `deploy_key_id`) plus `{known, key_id, kind}` for identify, and are cached 60 s positive and 5 s negative keyed by `(sha256(blob), repo, mode)`. `PublicApiAuthz` (Stage 1) answers `Unsupported` for both, and the U86 start check reads that to refuse `[ssh] enabled = true` with `provider = "forge"` and no internal endpoint. A 5xx, a timeout or a malformed answer denies (fail closed) and the forge's error body is never forwarded. Tests against `fake_forgejo`: a user key with read and with write permission on a repo; a private repo without access; a deploy key read-only and with write; a deploy key presented for another repo; an unknown key; a disabled or login-prohibited user; the cache and the negative-cache bound; the request carries the internal token and logs only the fingerprint (canary: the blob is never logged); Stage 1 `Unsupported`.

### U86 SSH listener and key authentication (`asc-serve::ssh`, feature `git-ssh`) [SPEC §25.11.1–§25.11.4, §25.11.6–§25.11.8, §25.11.11]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-serve/src/ssh/{mod.rs,server.rs,auth.rs,policy.rs,host_key.rs,limits.rs,audit.rs}`, `crates/asc-serve/tests/ssh_auth.rs`, `fuzz/fuzz_targets/ssh_command.rs`. **Recorded amendments:** workspace `Cargo.toml` (`russh`, exact version), `crates/asc-serve/Cargo.toml` and `crates/asc/Cargo.toml` (feature `git-ssh = ["git-compat", ...]`, default on, forwarding feature), U43c `crates/asc-serve/src/{config.rs,limits.rs}` (the `[ssh]` and `[limits.ssh]` tables, parsed in every build), `crates/asc-serve/src/lib.rs` (start the listener beside the HTTP server), the `asc serve ssh host-key show|rotate` subcommand lines (U43a table)
- **Depends:** U80, U78, M30, U44, U84, U69. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-serve --test ssh_auth && cargo check -p asc-serve --no-default-features`
- **Brief:** SPEC §25.11. A `russh` server on `[ssh] listen`, started only when the feature is built, `[ssh] enabled` and `[git] enabled` are true, and (with `provider = "forge"`) the internal endpoint exists; otherwise it is not bound and the reason is logged once. `auth.rs`: `publickey` only; the user name check; `identify_ssh_key` at the offer, `authorize_ssh_key` handed to the exec step; the key-type and RSA-size check; the same `Permission denied (publickey)` for every refusal. `policy.rs`: the command parser of SPEC §25.11.4 (a bounded hand-written parser, a fuzz target) and the request table (shell greeting and exit 1; subsystem, pty, x11, other env, direct-tcpip, tcpip-forward, streamlocal, tunnels and agent forwarding refused and audited). `host_key.rs`: the keys of §25.11.6 through `authz::key_file` (`create_private`, `open_private`, `check_private`), `show` and `rotate`. `limits.rs`: the caps of §25.11.7 and the connection admission before authentication (U69). `audit.rs`: the login, command and refused-request records and the `asc_ssh_*` metrics of §25.11.8. A valid pack command is handed to a `PackExec` trait object that U87 implements; the default answers `asc: git over ssh is not available` and exit 1, so this unit is testable alone. Tests (a `russh` client in the test crate): each refusal path gives the same message; password, keyboard-interactive and `none` refused; `ssh-rsa` with SHA-1, `ssh-dss` and a 1024-bit RSA key refused; the offered algorithm set contains no CBC, no SHA-1 key exchange, no compression, and offers `kex-strict-s-v00@openssh.com`; the command table (`git-upload-pack 'o/r.git'` and `'/o/r'` parse; `'../x'`, `'o/r;id'`, `'$(id)'`, a backtick, `'a'\''b'`, 257 bytes, NUL, a control byte, `git-upload-archive`, `scp -t`, `sftp`, `asc-authenticate` refused); every row of the request table; host key created once, kept across a restart with the same fingerprint, a group-readable file, a symlink and a foreign owner refused, a lost create race re-opens the winner's file, the private key is not in any log or `Debug` (canary); the 30 s authentication timeout, `max_auth_tries`, the unauthenticated caps (total and per peer), the per-peer connection rate and the keep-alive close; failed logins feed the same counters as HTTP; each login writes one audit record with the fingerprint and no key blob; `[ssh] enabled = true` with `[git] enabled = false`, and with `provider = "forge"` on `PublicApiAuthz`, does not bind and logs the reason; `cargo tree -p asc-serve --no-default-features` has no `russh`.

### U87 Git over SSH: upload-pack and receive-pack on the channel (`asc-git::compat::server_git`, `asc-serve::ssh::exec`) [SPEC §25.11.5]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-serve/src/ssh/exec.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/tests/ssh_git.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/tests/support/ssh_shim.rs` (a test-only `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` program built on the `russh` client, so real git can be the client without OpenSSH). **Recorded amendments:** U80 `crates/asc-git/src/compat/server_git.rs` (the session mode: no `--stateless-rpc`, full-duplex pipes, same environment and `-c` list), U81 `crates/asc-serve/src/git_http.rs` and U82 `crates/asc-serve/src/git_push.rs` (the transport-independent steps, namely admission, the access check, the gates, `GitView::sync` and the hook token, move into functions both transports call; HTTP behaviour and tests are unchanged), U86 `crates/asc-serve/src/ssh/mod.rs` (register the `PackExec` implementation)
- **Depends:** U81, U82, U86. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-serve --test ssh_git`
- **Brief:** SPEC §25.11.5. `PackExec` runs `git upload-pack <view>` or `git receive-pack <view>` through `ServerGit` in session mode, with `GIT_PROTOCOL` from the validated `env` request, child stdout to channel data, stderr to extended data and the exit code to `exit-status`; the process tree is killed at the deadline and at the idle limit. The push hook, `import_git_push` and `UpdateRefs` are U82's, unchanged, with `transport = ssh` in the audit record. Refusals are stderr text and exit 1 (`asc: repository not found` for a missing or unreadable repo, `asc: this key has read-only access`, the read-only mirror text, `asc: too many concurrent operations, retry later`). Tests with real git as the client through `ssh_shim`: `clone`, `fetch`, `ls-remote` in protocol v0, v1 and v2, `--depth`, `--filter=blob:none`; push of a new branch, a fast-forward, a deletion, a tag and `--atomic`; a user key reaches only its user's repos; a read deploy key clones and its push is refused; a write deploy key pushes; a missing repo and an unreadable one give the same text; the pre-receive path is the HTTP one (the user-ref line `remote: asc: stored as your branch`, branch protection messages, `-o land` refused) and the SHAs equal those from an HTTP clone of the same repo; hidden refs never appear; the 5th concurrent fetch and the 3rd concurrent push by one principal are refused; a stalled client is killed at the idle limit and a client that disconnects mid-push leaves no quarantine and no reachable asc object; a mirror-mode repo is read-only; no secret appears in a child's argv or environment (canary); bytes are charged to the `objects` budget; the audit record names the transport and the fingerprint.

### U88 SSH compatibility suite with real git and OpenSSH (`crates/asc/tests/git_ssh/`) [SPEC §25.11, §15.6.1, §17.1]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc/tests/git_ssh/{main.rs,openssh.rs,migrate.rs}`, `scripts/ci/no-ssh-weakening.sh`. **Recorded amendment:** U83 `scripts/ci/no-git-compat.sh` (adds the check that `cargo tree -e features -p asc --no-default-features` has no `russh`, and that a `git-compat` build without `git-ssh` binds no SSH port)
- **Depends:** U87, U85, U83, U79, U77. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc --test git_ssh && scripts/ci/no-ssh-weakening.sh` (real git >= 2.40 through `RealGit` and a real OpenSSH `ssh`, `ssh-keygen` and `ssh-agent`; the suite fails loudly in CI when they are missing and skips with a message locally)
- **Brief:** Proves SSH auth is git's, by running the real clients against an in-process `asc serve` (token store) with the listener on an ephemeral port. The test `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` sets only `-p`, `-i`, `-o IdentitiesOnly=yes` and a `UserKnownHostsFile` pre-seeded with the server's host key, so host-key checking stays on. Cases: `clone`, `fetch`, `pull`, `push` (new branch, fast-forward, delete, tag, `--atomic`, `--mirror`), shallow and partial clones, protocol v0, v1 and v2; ed25519, ECDSA and RSA-3072 keys, a key in `ssh-agent`, a `~/.ssh/config` `Host` alias with `Port` and `IdentityFile`; GitHub-style `git@host:owner/repo.git` and `ssh://git@host:port/owner/repo.git`; `asc clone`, `asc fetch` and `asc push` against the same URLs in git-remote mode. **Refusals:** `ssh -T git@host` prints the no-shell greeting and exits 1; `ssh git@host id`, `sh -c id`, `git-upload-archive`, `scp` and `sftp` are refused; `ssh -L`, `-R`, `-D`, `-A`, `-X` and `-N` are refused and the server is unaffected; an unregistered key, a revoked key, a wrong user name and a deploy key on the wrong repo get `Permission denied (publickey)` or the repo-not-found text and git exits 128. **Host keys:** a changed host key makes git, and `asc fetch`, fail with `REMOTE HOST IDENTIFICATION HAS CHANGED` and neither overrides it; an unknown host with `BatchMode` fails with `Host key verification failed`; under `Prompt::Never` the child ssh has no terminal. **No weakening:** a recording `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` script shows that the argv of ssh started by `asc` equals the argv git itself produces for the same URL (no `-o`, no `StrictHostKeyChecking`, no `UserKnownHostsFile`), `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` and `core.sshCommand` from trusted config are honoured, and `core.sshCommand` in a repo config owned by another user is ignored (SPEC §15.6.4 item 2). `scripts/ci/no-ssh-weakening.sh` fails when the Rust sources, scripts or generated config name `StrictHostKeyChecking`, `UserKnownHostsFile`, `CheckHostIP` or `VerifyHostKeyDNS` outside an allow-list of the test files that assert their absence. **Forge:** against `fake_forgejo` (U85) a Forgejo user key and a deploy key. **Whole stack:** each login and command writes one audit record with no key material (canary: the private key bytes never appear in logs); admission sheds over-limit logins and fetches; the branchless cases of SPEC §25.6 over SSH.

### U64 Actions, ASC Actions fork and ASC workflows
- **Owns [audit: actions-repo-naming, actions-fork-contract-and-naming]:** `actions/{checkout,cache,upload-artifact,download-artifact}/action.yml`, `actions/asc/{setup,changed,affected,turbo-cache}/action.yml` (the SPEC §12.7 action set; `asc/turbo-cache` exports `TURBO_API/TOKEN/TEAM/CACHE` for jobs without checkout), `actions/test/selftest.sh`, `deploy/runner/{asc-warm.service,asc-warm.timer,runner-config-snippet.yaml}`, `docs/ci/asc-workflows.patch` (proposed changes to `D:\dev\monorepo\.forgejo\workflows\*`, applied by ASC as a PR, not by this unit), `docs/ci/asc-projects.toml`
- **Depends:** U62, U63, U49, U72, U75. **Accept:** `bash actions/test/selftest.sh` (starts a local asc serve, runs the actions' commands in a container with a read-only host cache mount, asserts the synthetic `.git`, `TURBO_SCM_BASE` and outputs)
- **Brief:** Composite actions that install `asc` (cached by version) and call `asc ci …`, keeping upstream's inputs and outputs (`ref`, `fetch-depth`, `path`, `submodules`, `lfs`, `sparse-checkout`) mapped to asc semantics (SPEC §12.7); `checkout` falls back to upstream git checkout for repos that are not asc. Runner config for the host cache read-only bind mount and the warm timer. The workflow patch: a plan job (`asc ci changed` + `asc ci affected`, outputs `rust`, `matrix`, `turbo_filter`, `count`), verify/rust/images/e2e jobs gated on outputs, no `fetch-depth: 0`, no `submodules: recursive`, `TURBO_API` to asc serve, a `green/master` post-merge job and a deploy gate on `green/*` (SPEC §12.6). `docs/ci/asc-projects.toml` proposes an ASC `.asc/projects.toml` (tags `image`/`deploy`; CI workflow files as global inputs).

## §C Stack track: stacked changes and stacked PRs (SPEC §20) [audit: stacks-design-not-folded, stacks-design-exists-not-folded]

Source: `docs/research/stacks.md` §6, with its unit ids S01–S52 renamed to **C01–C52** (S-ids collide with the spikes S1–S12 of SPEC §19, for example `S12` was both a spike and `asc absorb`). Stack verbs live under `crates/asc/src/stack/`; every unit owns its own test file (`crates/asc/tests/stack_<name>.rs`), and none is shared. C07 owns only `crates/asc/src/stack/cli/mod.rs`, which uses `automod::dir!` and a `linkme` distributed slice, so each verb unit adds its own file and registers itself without touching `mod.rs`. Ids are stable: C16 is withdrawn and not reused.

**Phases (SPEC §18):**
- **1c Local stacks:** C01–C15 and C51, after U26/U27c/U27d (C04 also after U32). C05–C15 run in every mode, with no server.
- **4s Server stacks:** C17–C32, C45, C49, C50 and C52, after U47/U66/U68 (C17, C23, C29 and C30a–C30d can start earlier, as their dependencies allow). C33 is v1.x.
- **9s Forgejo stacks:** C34–C43 and C46–C48 on the Go track after K17. C44 is v1.x.

**Recorded amendments.** These hunks go into files that another unit owns. The C unit makes the hunk, and the hunk is recorded in the owning unit's Owns list by this table; it is allowed like the `mod` registration.

| Owning unit and file | Hunk | Made by |
|---|---|---|
| U20 `crates/asc/src/ext.rs` | `RewriteSink`/`OpSink` with no-op defaults (already in U20's brief, so U20 lands them) | U20 |
| `asc-core`/`asc-store` key enums (built, U04/U07) | `ObjKey::ChangeMeta` (`m/`), envelope kind 6 (CARRY.md) | C01 |
| U33 `crates/asc-git/src/import.rs` | Call `import_change::write_meta` for each imported commit | C45 |

## §B Branchless track: user refs, commit cloud and the pre-upload guard (SPEC §21) [audit: branchless-folded]

Source: `docs/research/branchless.md` §7, with the review fixes it records. B20 is withdrawn (folded into C01 and then into B18a) and is not reused. Branchless code lives in `crates/asc/src/branchless/` (`mod.rs` owned by B01), `crates/asc-engine/src/branchless/` (`mod.rs` owned by B04) and `crates/asc-serve/src/cloud/` (`mod.rs` owned by B09). The first unit to land creates a missing `mod.rs`; each later unit adds its own `pub mod` line to it (the general exception). Every unit owns its test files, and none is shared.

**Phases (SPEC §18):** the local units B01–B06 and B24 are phase 1c (after C04/C05); B07–B28 are phase 4b (after U47/U48d/U53b/C24). B17, B23 and B27 run on the Forgejo and CI tracks. B22 needs the MCP units M03 and M10 (`docs/research/mcp.md`), which the MCP fold adds to this plan; B22 cannot start before they exist.

**Recorded amendments.** These hunks go into files that another unit owns. The B unit makes the hunk; it is allowed like the `mod` registration.

| B29 | Live restack and update executor (`op kind live`, safety gates, caps) | C06, C10, C15, B01, B04 | `crates/asc-engine/src/branchless/live.rs` | `cargo test -p asc-engine branchless::live` | SPEC §21.3.8. Takes plain inputs (trunk tip, visible own drafts, worktree state) and returns a `LivePlan` (fetch only, restack, restack and move, or a `Notice`). Restack runs in memory (C06) and applies only when every restack is conflict-free. The move is refused while the tree is dirty (including untracked files that would be overwritten), an operation is in progress (rebase, merge, cherry-pick, bisect), conflicts are unresolved or the asc index lock is held. Each applied plan is one op-log entry of kind `live` (C03/C04). It refuses over `asc.live.maxStack` and `asc.live.maxFiles`. Runs no hooks. Tests: a dirty tree is never moved (tracked edit and a colliding untracked file); each in-progress operation blocks the move; `asc undo` reverts a live entry; other users' drafts are ignored; a conflicting plan restacks nothing and returns a notice; over-cap input returns a notice. |

A scheduler task per repo: subscribes with `WatchRefs` for `master`, `release/*` and consumes `Watch KIND_CLOUD` for own drafts; debounces and coalesces (`asc.live.debounce`) and runs the B29 plan for each worktree at most once per window; falls back to `ListRefs` polling (`asc.live.pollInterval`, backoff) when the stream is unavailable; reuses the daemon credential path; all fetched objects go through sync verification. Tests (virtual clock): a burst of events inside the window runs one update; events for other refs or other users' drafts do nothing; the poll fallback starts when the stream fails and the stream resumes with the cursor; a paused worktree is skipped; a stream error never blocks `asc.live.fetch`-only mode. |
| B31 | `asc live pause\|resume\|status` | B30, C07 | `crates/asc/src/cli/live.rs`, `crates/asc/tests/live_cli.rs` | `cargo test -p asc --test live_cli` | SPEC §21.3.8. Asc-only verbs through the daemon IPC; `status` prints per-worktree mode, last run and pause reason with `--json`. `asc status` and `asc sl` print the live-conflict notice (recorded hunks in the U21a and C08 files) until `resume`. Tests: pause survives a daemon restart; `resume` clears the notice; with no daemon the verbs fail with the daemon-absent message; the notice shows in `asc status` and `asc sl`. |

## §M MCP track: the asc JSON profile, `asc mcp`, `asc mcp --remote` and `asc serve /mcp`, forge-neutral (SPEC §22) [audit: mcp-folded; revised v1.10]

**Forge-neutral (v1.10, user decisions).** MCP is an asc feature. It works against any asc deployment, authenticates through the same `Authorizer` as git (forge delegation or the standalone token store), and the Forgejo fork carries no MCP code. The former Forgejo units M20–M28 are SUPERSEDED (table at the end of this section) and build nothing. The asc-side units that cover the forge-neutral design are: server core M03–M05; `asc mcp` stdio M09; `asc serve /mcp` transport M12a and PRM/401/403 M13; authorizer integration M29 and M30; remote proxy M31; tool wiring M08, M12b, M15 and M32; optional OAuth M33; optional forge toolset M34 (v1.x).

Source: `docs/research/mcp.md` §8, with the review fixes it records (SPEC §22.9 lists the rulings made at the fold). The unit ids are mcp.md's own, so `docs/research/ide-plugins.md` (P33b, P42, P50) and `docs/research/codeintel.md` (I21) keep pointing at the right units. Code lives in `crates/asc-mcp/` (the protocol layer, no repository logic; `Cargo.toml`, `build.rs`, `tools.toml` and `src/lib.rs` are owned by M03), `crates/asc/src/mcp/` (`mod.rs` owned by M09), `crates/asc-serve/src/mcp/` (`mod.rs` owned by M12a) and `crates/asc-engine/src/render/` (`mod.rs` owned by M04a). The first unit to land creates a missing `mod.rs`; each later unit adds its own `pub mod` line (the general exception). Every unit owns its test files, and none is shared. Dependencies: `rmcp = { version = "3.6.0", default-features = false, features = ["server", "base64", "transport-io", "transport-async-rw", "transport-streamable-http-server", "elicitation", "request-state"] }` is added by M03 as its one `[workspace.dependencies]` line (SPEC §22.7.1: no `macros`, no `schemars`, no `auth`). The stdio acceptance tests are inspector-style: an rmcp client (a dev-dependency with `client` and `transport-child-process`) spawns `asc mcp` and runs `server/discover`, `tools/list` and tool calls against a fixture repo.

**Phases (SPEC §18):** M12a–M14 and M18 are phase 4m (after U47, U69, C19, G09); M01a–M11 and M15–M17 are phase 5m (after U73, C31, C24, C25); M29 and M30 join phase 4m (M30 only needs U43a and U42 and can start early); M31–M33 are phase 9m (M34 is v1.x); M20–M28 are superseded. B22 (the branchless tools of SPEC §21.7) needs M03 and M10, so it moved to wave 30. Critical path: M01a → M01b → M01c → M02 → M03 → M04 → M05 → M08 → M09 (the local read surface, which works in local-only mode) → M10. M04a runs in parallel with M01a–M03 and gates M04. The server path is M05 → M12a → M12b → M29 → M13 → M14 (M18 before M14; M30 is independent). M31, M32 and M33 follow M13. M06, M07, M09b and M17 run in parallel after their dependencies.

**Recorded amendments.** These hunks go into files that another unit owns. The M unit makes the hunk; it is allowed like the `mod` registration. U41 is built, so its briefs are not rewritten: M01a and M01b are its follow-up units.

Each unit is done under the general rules (acceptance passes, clippy green, only owned files changed). The SPEC sections named are normative.

| ID | Title | Depends | Owns | Accept | Brief and required tests |

| M01a | `options.proto`, the JSON profile codec, descriptor export | U41 | `proto/asc/v1/options.proto`, `crates/asc-rpc/src/json.rs`; amendment: `build.rs` and `lib.rs` of U41 (descriptor set) | `cargo test -p asc-rpc json:: && buf lint proto` | SPEC §22.4.2. `extend google.protobuf.FieldOptions { JsonKind json = 50101; }` with kinds `HEX`, `CHANGE`, `PATH`, `TEXT`, `DATA`. `asc_rpc::json` encodes and decodes a `DynamicMessage` by descriptor (decoding accepts exactly what encoding prints; enums also accept the full name), and exports the constant `json.profile.v1`. **Superseded Forgejo units (v1.10).** M20–M28 were the Forgejo fork's MCP (`/api/mcp`, its tools and its MCP OAuth). They are superseded by M-D1, M-D11 and M-D12 (SPEC §22.1, §22.9) and build nothing. Their state in `D:\devorgejo` (branch `asc/v16`) is: M20 is committed as `28ceff203` (9 files, 1,866 lines: `routers/api/mcp/{server,transport,auth}.go` with tests, `modules/setting/mcp.go`, a line in `routers/init.go`); M26 is uncommitted and was stopped (a diff of 9 modified files, 363 insertions, plus untracked files: `routers/web/auth/oauth_asc_mcp.go`, `services/oauth2_provider/`, `models/forgejo_migrations/v17d_asc_oauth_resource.go` and tests, `services/auth/method/oauth2_audience_test.go`, `routers/api/mcp/resource_test.go`; it also edits `routers/private/asc.go`, the K07 authz endpoint). M21–M25, M27 and M28 were never started. **Recommendation (a):** the user approves reverting `28ceff203` and discarding the M26 working tree, leaving the fork with no MCP; `K07` keeps its pre-M26 answer unless M33 or P06 needs an `aud` field (then that hunk alone is kept, as a K07 change). Option (b), a thin forge-only adapter, is not recommended for v1; if issue or PR-comment tools are wanted, build M34 in `asc serve`. Nothing in the fork is reverted by this document.

## §I Code-intelligence track: search, symbols and vectors (SPEC §23) [audit: codeintel-folded]

Source: `docs/research/codeintel.md` §9, with the review fixes it records (SPEC §23.12 lists the rulings made at the fold). The unit ids are codeintel.md's own, so `docs/research/mcp.md` (M15) and `docs/research/ide-plugins.md` (P09, P23) keep pointing at the right units. Code lives in `crates/asc-codeintel/` (`Cargo.toml` and `src/lib.rs` are owned by I01; every later unit adds its own `pub mod` line, the general exception), `crates/asc-embed/` (I09), `crates/asc-serve/src/codeintel/` (`mod.rs` owned by I13), `crates/asc/src/cli/codeintel/` (I17), `crates/asc/src/codeintel_local/` (I19) and the Forgejo fork (I23–I26). Dependencies (pin exactly; `cargo deny` must pass): `tantivy 0.27.0`, `regex 1.13.1`, `regex-syntax 0.8.11`, `roaring` (current 0.10.x, pinned at I05), `tree-sitter 0.27.1`, `tree-sitter-tags 0.27.1`, `tree-sitter-language 0.1.9` with the grammars named in I03, `fastembed 7.2.0` (`default-features = false`), `ort =2.0.0-rc.15`, `usearch 2.26.5` (feature `ann`), `blake3`. Every unit owns its test files; none is shared.

**Phases (SPEC §18):** I01–I17, I22 and I29 are phase 4c (after U47, U43c, U69, U44, C19); I21 is phase 4c-MCP (after I16, M15, M08); I18–I20 are phase 5c (after U53b, U73); I23–I26 are phase 9c on the Go track (after K10 and I16); I27 and I28 are O-track; I30 and I31 are v1.x. Waves: see the parallel execution summary; I23–I26 are on the Go track and have no wave.

**Recorded amendments.** These hunks go into files that another unit owns. U41 and U14b are built and are not touched.

Ranking window 2,000 files; cursor `(window, ord)`. |

## §P IDE plugins track: bridge, `asc-ui`, VS Code and JetBrains (SPEC §24) [audit: ide-plugins-folded]

Source: `docs/research/ide-plugins.md` §7, with the review fixes it records (SPEC §24.10 lists the rulings made at the fold). The unit ids are the research file's own (P01a–P56), so `docs/research/mcp.md` and `docs/research/codeintel.md` keep pointing at the right units; P57 and P58 are added at the fold for the API hunks the research file left unowned. Code lives in `proto/asc/v1/bridge.proto` and `crates/asc/src/bridge/` (P01a–P01c), `packages/{asc-client-ts,asc-ui,asc-ui-devhost,asc-fixtures}` (TypeScript), `plugins/vscode` (TypeScript), `plugins/jetbrains` (Kotlin, Gradle) and the Forgejo fork (P04–P07, P58). The TypeScript, Kotlin and Go rules are in the rules list at the top of this file.

**Phases (SPEC §18):** P01a–P03, P45, P46 and P57 are phase 5p (after U50, U52, M02, M09, U73, C31); P04–P07 and P58 are phase 9p on the Go track (after K06, K07, K14, C40, C41b); P08–P35c, P44a, P47a–P47c, P49a, P50 and the `asc-ui` views P13–P23 and P56 are phase 10 (VS Code); P36–P43b, P44b, P48, P49b and P51–P55 are phase 10j (JetBrains). Waves: see the parallel execution summary; the Go units have no wave.

**Recorded amendments.** These hunks go into files that another unit owns. U41 is built, so the hunks in its files are made by the follow-up unit P57 and its brief is not rewritten.

| P01b | `Bridge.Run`: per-verb flag schema, argv rules of SPEC §24.4.5.1, rebuilt environment, no stdin, typed `OpResult` | P01a, P45 | `crates/asc/src/bridge/{run.rs,argv_schema.rs}`, `crates/asc/tests/bridge_run.rs` | `cargo test -p asc --test bridge_run` | SPEC §24.4.5.1 (argv rules). `Bridge.Run`: the embedded per-verb flag schema (`argv_schema.rs`, a test checks it against `allowlist.json`), the six argv rules, `--end-of-options` before positionals (A7: the hunk in the CLI argument frame must make every allowlisted verb accept it), `ASC_NO_ALIASES=1` (A5, hunk in alias resolution), rebuilt child environment, null stdin, at most 4 children per bridge, stderr kept to 1 MiB, cancel kills the child. Output decodes through `asc_rpc::json` into `OpResult`. Required test: the injection corpus (`-c`, `-C`, `--exec`, `--exec-path`, aliases, `-x` after `--`, values starting with `-`, stdin attempts) is rejected, and every allowlisted verb has a `--json` form. |
| P01c | `Bridge.Token`/`StoreToken`: non-interactive credential calls, `NOT_FOUND`, `NO_CREDENTIAL_HELPER`, `ASC_TOKEN` precedence | P01a | `crates/asc/src/bridge/auth.rs`, `crates/asc/tests/bridge_auth.rs` | `cargo test -p asc --test bridge_auth` | SPEC §24.6. `Bridge.Token` and `Bridge.StoreToken`: `ASC_TOKEN`, else `git credential fill` for the forge host with `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, `GCM_INTERACTIVE=never`, `GIT_ASKPASS` unset and no editor, so it never opens a dialog; no credential gives `NOT_FOUND`. `StoreToken` first checks that `credential.helper` is set and is not `store`; otherwise it fails with `NO_CREDENTIAL_HELPER` and writes nothing. A host-supplied `ASC_TOKEN` wins over an inherited one. `Token` never prints to stdout unrequested and is never relayed to a webview. |
| P02 | `Workspace.ReadFile` (rev, file id, stage set; offline from the local store) | U73, C31 | `crates/asc/src/daemon/plugin_api_read.rs`, `crates/asc/tests/plugin_api_read.rs` | `cargo test -p asc --test plugin_api_read` | SPEC §7 (`Workspace.ReadFile`), §24.4.2. Streams a file at `rev:path`, a `FileId` or a stage (0–3) from the local store, offline; it reads the store, never the working tree, and hydrates through the tiers only when an object is missing (IP-D14). Recorded hunk: the RPC and its messages in U73's `workspace.proto`, and one row in `docs/plugin-api.md`. |

Redirects: `vscode://asc.asc/auth`, `cursor://asc.asc/auth`, `vscodium://asc.asc/auth`, and `http://127.0.0.1:<any port>/…` (RFC 8252 §7.3). Scopes `read:user`, `read:repository`, `write:repository`, `read:issue`, `write:issue`. Not used for MCP; the resource-indicator work of M26 is not required. |

---

## §K Forgejo fork track (Go, `D:\dev\forgejo`, branch `asc/v16`)

Each unit is one commit on the patch stack (SPEC §13.3). Commands run from `D:\dev\forgejo`. A Go engineer owns this track. It needs Rust units only where noted, as `ASC_SERVER_BIN` **[audit: k-track-deps]**: `ASC_SERVER_BIN` = `<source-control>/target/release/asc` built by `cargo build -p asc --release --no-default-features --features serve`. The Go tests start it with `serve --config <tmp> --db sqlite::memory: --store memory://`.

| K19 | Internal SSH key lookup (extends K07; SPEC §25.11.3): `POST /api/internal/asc/ssh-key` identifies a key, and the authz endpoint accepts `ssh_key` in place of a token. Thin: it calls the helpers `routers/private/serv.go` (`ServCommand`) already uses for user keys, deploy keys and repository permission, adds no table, stores no key in asc and changes no SSH behaviour of Forgejo itself | `routers/private/asc_sshkey.go`, hunks in `routers/private/asc.go` (request variant), `routers/private/internal.go` (one route line) and `modules/private/asc.go` (the two request and response structs) | K07 | `go test ./routers/private/... -run TestAscSSHKey` (user key with read and write permission, private repo without access, deploy key read-only and with write, deploy key on another repo, unknown key, inactive or login-prohibited user, internal token required, `[asc] ENABLED` off gives 404) |
| K17 | Relaxed approvals by change class [audit: 27-semantic-classification] | `models/git/protected_branch.go` fields plus a migration, `services/pull/review_asc.go`, `templates/repo/diff/asc_class_badge.tmpl` | K10, U67 | `go test ./services/pull/... -run TestAscRelaxedApprovals` |

**Runner fork track** (Go, `D:\dev\forgejo-runner`, one commit per unit) [audit: user decision Actions fork]:

| ID | Title | Owns | Depends | Accept |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R01 | Action alias table | `internal/app/run/alias.go`, `config.example.yaml` hunk | – | `go test ./internal/app/run/... -run TestActionAlias` |
| R02 | Native checkout fast path | `internal/app/run/asc_checkout.go` | R01, U62 | `go test ./internal/app/run/... -run TestAscCheckoutNative` |

**Actions mirror** (`asc/actions` on Forgejo):

| ID | Title | Owns | Depends | Accept |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A01 | `asc/actions` mirror | the `asc/actions` repo: `checkout`, `cache`, `upload-artifact`, `download-artifact`, `asc/setup`, `asc/changed`, `asc/affected`, `asc/turbo-cache` (from U64's `actions/`), plus `deploy/forgejo/actions.ini.snippet` (`[actions] DEFAULT_ACTIONS_URL` pointing at the mirror) | U64 | `bash actions/test/selftest.sh --mirror <url>` |

**Briefs:**

- **K02–K05:** follow `docs/research/forgejo.md` §4.2 phases 1–4 exactly. No behaviour change. The full existing test suite must stay green at every commit.
- **K03:** `Backend` is the method set in `forgejo.md` §2 Seam A. The `gitcli` code moves mostly verbatim. Add `build/rebase-map.sh` (SPEC §13.3).
- **K06:** an `ascclient.Client` with a connection pool, bearer auth (the user token is forwarded for user-initiated calls; the internal token for system calls), retries on `UNAVAILABLE`, and an LRU of decoded objects keyed by immutable id (default 256 MiB).
- **K07:** the response schema is exactly SPEC §13.3 K07. It reuses the `/serv/command` permission logic. The Actions task token resolves the run and event (`is_fork_pr` from the run's PR). `branch_rules.require_signed` comes from the protected-branch settings.
- **K09:** pre-receive asc branch: after the existing checks, call `ImportGitPush` with `GIT_QUARANTINE_PATH`, `GIT_OBJECT_DIRECTORY` and `GIT_ALTERNATE_OBJECT_DIRECTORIES` from the hook env; on error, reject with asc's message. Post-receive: for `storage_backend=asc` repos, ignore calls whose `PushTrigger != "asc"`, because asc's outbox is the single fan-out. `HookOptions` gains nothing new; `PushTrigger` already exists.
- **K10:** map every `Backend` method to the RPCs in SPEC §13.3 K10 with `id_mode=GIT` while `object_format=sha1`. Batch `LastCommits` and `Stat`. Language stats are computed in Go over `ReadTree` plus `ReadFile`.
- **K11:** sampled comparison (`[asc] SHADOW_SAMPLE=0.1`) of `GetCommit`, `GetTree`, `ListRefs`, `Log` pages, `GetDiff` name-status, `GetMergeBase`, blame and `Merge(dry_run)` vs `git merge-tree`. Metrics plus logs; the response always comes from gitcli.
- **K12:** each `Writer` method = one RPC. PR head refs use `CopyClosure` then `UpdateRefs refs/pull/<n>/head`. AGit pushes (Stage ≥ 4) go through asc's proc-receive call path (SPEC §13.5): `refs/for/*` is never stored; Forgejo returns `refs/pull/<n>/head`, which asc updates with `SKIP_PRE`.
- **K09 (AGit, Stages 2–3):** patch the internal writers of `refs/pull/*` (proc-receive handling, PR head sync) to call asc `UpdateRefs(refs/pull/<n>/head, SKIP_PRE)` first and write the bridge only through `bridge_promote`. An asc-serve test `agit::proc_receive_sets_pull_head` (in U48d's `import_push.rs` test module) covers the server side.
- **K17:** branch protection fields `asc_relaxed_classes`/`asc_relaxed_approvals`; a PR whose files are all in relaxed classes (from `Classify`) needs the relaxed approval count; protected-file globs always override; a per-file class badge.
- **R01/R02:** the alias table maps `actions/checkout@*`, `actions/cache@*`, `actions/upload-artifact@*`, `actions/download-artifact@*` to `asc/actions/*`; the fast path recognises the checkout action and runs `asc ci checkout` on the host with `ASC_HOME=<job dir>/asc-home`, bind-mounting the tree, that `ASC_HOME` and the `asc` binary directory into the job container at identical absolute paths, then sets `GITHUB_ENV`/`GITHUB_PATH` as SPEC §12.3 step 6 (SPEC §12.7) [audit: ci-later-steps-asc-home-and-runner-mounts]. Test `TestAscCheckoutNative` runs `git rev-parse HEAD` inside the container.
- **K13:** `bridgePath(repo)` everywhere a repo path served git. A lazy bridge build calls `asc export --git <bridge>` (the `asc` binary on the host) on the first legacy request (a request-scoped lock). For `storage_backend=asc` repos the LFS endpoints are disabled (No LFS, SPEC §2.5); historic LFS content is archived outside asc. Fork, delete and size use `RepoAdmin`.
- **K14:** `paths:` matching uses `Diff(NAMES)` between before and after (push) or merge base and head (PR). The context gains the SPEC fields.

## Ops units

### O01 GC (two-mark, fenced)
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-store/src/gc.rs`, `crates/asc-serve/src/gc.rs`, `crates/asc/src/cli/gc_store.rs`
- **Depends:** U43b. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-store gc:: && cargo test -p asc-serve gc::`
- **Brief:** SPEC §3.6: marks from refs plus recent changesets and `ref_log` targets within `asc.gc.pitrDays`, the two-mark and 14-day rule, the server's `gc_marks` table, bucket marks under `gc/<run>/` with a `gc/lock` lease, dry-run output, extra roots from a `GcRootSource` trait defined here (U75 implements it for unexpired Actions manifests, SPEC §3.6) [audit: actions-cache-artifact-underspecified], and **sweep fencing** [audit: gc-sweep-race-and-pitr-roots]: the exclusive advisory lock per sweep batch with re-mark (server) and `gc/freeze` (bucket). Tests: an in-flight push across marks keeps re-referenced old objects; unreachable old objects are deleted only after the second mark; concurrent GC runs are refused; `gc::sweep_race_push_after_mark2`; `gc::pitr_restore_closure`.

### O02 Release and distribution
- **Owns:** `.forgejo/workflows/release.yml`, `packaging/windows/{main.wxs,register-task.ps1,defender.ps1}`, `packaging/winget/**`, `packaging/linux/{install.sh,asc-daemon.service}`, `crates/asc/src/cli/upgrade.rs`
- **Depends:** U53c, U20a. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc upgrade:: && cargo wix --nocapture --package asc` (on Windows CI)
- **Brief:** SPEC §16.2: release builds for 4 targets; the MSI (installs to a versioned directory behind a `current` junction, always creates the private shim dir `<ver>\shim`, and the optional "Use asc for git commands" shim: an elevated install inserts `<prefix>\current\shim` into the **machine** PATH immediately before `Git\cmd` (SPEC §11.4) [audit: windows-shim-path-precedence-and-location], plus the `~/.bashrc` block for Git Bash; enables Client-ProjFS, adds Defender exclusions, registers the logon task; a WiX test asserts that `where git` lists the shim first); a winget manifest; the Linux installer (versioned layout, optional shim) and user unit; `asc upgrade` with minisign verification, side-by-side install, shim relinking and the daemon `Shutdown`; the daily update check; `Version.min_client` enforcement messages. **Release gate:** the compat suite (SPEC §17.1) is green on Windows and Linux. Tests: upgrade refuses a tampered artefact; `upgrade::side_by_side_relinks_helpers` (Windows CI).

### O03 Migration runbook and admin verify
- **Owns:** `docs/runbooks/{asc-cutover.md,rollback.md,restore.md}`, `crates/asc-serve/src/admin_cli.rs`
- **Depends:** U45, K09. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-serve admin_cli::` [audit: acceptance-ambiguity]
- **Brief:** SPEC §14.3 as an executable runbook with exact commands; `asc serve admin import` (server-side import, the only way git history reaches a server, SPEC §8.4), `asc serve admin verify-import` (every git commit's export equals git; skipped refs of SPEC §14.1 step 6 are excluded from the mismatch count) [audit: import-non-commit-tags], `verify --all` (closure and git map), `outbox drain --wait`, and `set-mode`. The rollback and restore drills are scripted. The staging run (`asc serve admin verify-import --repo asc/dds --repo asc/soma` exits 0 with `mismatches: 0`) is a cutover gate in the runbook, not the unit's acceptance.

### O04 Developer docs and cheat sheet
- **Owns:** `docs/CHEATSHEET.md`, `docs/ONBOARDING.md`, `docs/SCION-TRACEABILITY.md`, `docs/agents/claude-code-worktrees.md`, `crates/asc/src/cli/help_git.rs`
- **Depends:** U40, U54w. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc help_git::` (the cheat sheet embedded in `asc help git` matches `docs/CHEATSHEET.md`; the sibling test `traceability_rows` asserts that every checklist number 1–32 has a row in SPEC §1.1)
- **Brief:** The git → asc mapping (SPEC §11.3); onboarding (install, the `git` shim and its surfaces — PATH order, Git Bash `~/.bashrc`, turbo's environment; `clone --reference`, reclaim disk; editor settings for search, watcher excludes and language servers per SPEC §10.2.5a; the branch-scope model, SPEC §6.1; the `.gitattributes` and checkout-option deviations, SPEC §2.4.1, §11.5); Claude Code `WorktreeCreate`/`WorktreeRemove` hook configuration and the `.claude/settings.json` snippet (spike S7); a troubleshooting section keyed by `asc doctor` findings. IDE git integrations are documented as unsupported; ASC's plugins use the API (U73).

### O05 Benchmark gate: git vs asc (`bench/`) [audit: user decisions benchmarks and checkout speed]
- **Owns:** `bench/**` (hyperfine scenario scripts, a criterion suite for internals, the deterministic repo generator `bench/gen`, `bench/baseline.json`), `.forgejo/workflows/bench.yml`, `docs/BENCHMARKS.md`
- **Depends:** U58, G08, U62. **Accept:** `cargo run -p asc-bench --release -- --suite quick` (runs git and asc on the small workload and a 10x synthetic repo, prints the table, and fails when asc loses a Scion-model scenario, is > 10% slower elsewhere, or regresses > 20% vs `bench/baseline.json`)
- **Brief:** SPEC §16.4: scenarios (clone full/blobless/shallow/sparse/scalar for git vs asc virtual and plain, `worktree add`, `status` clean and dirty, `switch`, `diff` HEAD/merge-base/`--stat`, path-limited `log`, `blame`, `merge-base`/`is-ancestor`, `commit`/`push`/`fetch`, CI checkout plus affected, turbo `--dry`), workloads (small, medium, HUGE; `bench/gen` with 1M-file and 100 GB variants for the nightly run), metrics (wall p50/p95, time to first usable shell and first build, bytes transferred and on disk, files hydrated, peak RSS, server CPU; cold and warm), daemon idle RSS ≤ 64 MiB, and the published `docs/BENCHMARKS.md` table per release. The medium and HUGE clone scenarios run with the default history window (SPEC §6.3) [audit: clone-history-o-history]; the HUGE scenarios run with `asc.turboIndex` on and off, reporting index render time separately (SPEC §16.4) [audit: synthetic-index-o-files]. Every real-git invocation in `bench/` passes `-c init.defaultBranch=master` (U01b's lint covers `bench/`) [audit: no-main-lint-misses-common-forms].

### O06 Observability catalogue [audit: 29-observability-superfans]
- **Owns:** `docs/metrics.md`, `deploy/alerts.yml`, `deploy/grafana/*.json`, `docs/runbooks/tier-*.md`, `crates/asc/src/telemetry.rs`
- **Depends:** U43c, U53b, U63. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc telemetry:: && promtool check rules deploy/alerts.yml`
- **Brief:** SPEC §16.1 and §16.4: shared log fields, OTLP init and the event catalogue (`telemetry.rs`), the metric catalogue, alert rules (including the three-day cache-SLO rule), dashboards and per-tier runbooks. A test asserts that every metric name in `docs/metrics.md` is registered by the binary.

### O07 Fuzz targets [audit: 24-fuzzing]
- **Owns:** `fuzz/**`, `.forgejo/workflows/fuzz.yml`
- **Depends:** U04, U05, U13, U16, U41, U48a, G03–G06. **Accept:** `bash fuzz/run-all.sh 60` (runs every target for 60 s; non-zero on a crash)
- **Brief:** SPEC §17.3 targets, regressions under `fuzz/regressions/<target>/` replayed by `cargo test`, PR (60 s per touched crate) and nightly (30 min) schedules.

### O08 Simulation harness (`asc-sim`) [audit: 24-hermetic-simulation]
- **Owns:** `crates/asc-sim/**`
- **Depends:** U46, U48d, O01, U65, U70, U01b. **Accept:** `cargo test -p asc-sim -- --seeds 200`
- **Brief:** SPEC §17.2: turmoil-based replicas (primary and cache role), clients, fake Forgejo and in-memory store; `Faulty<O>`; named crash points; the scenarios and invariants listed there; seed printing and replay; PR and nightly seed counts.
